The 23rd China-ASEAN Expo opened on September 17, 2026, in Nanning, Guangxi, featuring a new &\#x27;AI Megamall&\#x27; where over 70 enterprises showcased more than 500 consumer AI products for hands-on experience. The event coincided with the opening of the Pinglu Canal, described as a new &\#x27;logistics lifeline&\#x27; connecting China and ASEAN. Trade between the two regions reached 5.95 trillion yuan in the first eight months of 2026, a 20.6% year-on-year increase, with China remaining ASEAN&\#x27;s largest trading partner for the 17th consecutive year. The expo&\#x27;s theme, &\#x27;Sharing the 3.0 Opportunities,&\#x27; aligned with the implementation of the China-ASEAN Free Trade Area 3.0 upgrade, which focuses on digital economy, green economy, and AI cooperation. In the first half of 2026, China exported 2.355 million new energy vehicles, a 120% year-on-year increase, with strong demand from the ASEAN market.

**「Context」** The China-ASEAN Free Trade Area 3.0 upgrade, signed to enhance regional economic integration, places new emphasis on digital economy, green and blue economies, and artificial intelligence. The Pinglu Canal, the first man-made canal in China since the founding of the People&\#x27;s Republic that connects a river to the sea, represents a major infrastructure breakthrough designed to boost &\#x27;hard connectivity&\#x27; and logistics between China and ASEAN nations.

**「Consequence」** For ASEAN businesses, the combination of the Pinglu Canal&\#x27;s logistical improvements and the expanding China-ASEAN AI and digital infrastructure aims to lower the application threshold for AI services and reduce costs for local companies. The surge in Chinese new energy vehicle exports to ASEAN also signals a shifting market dynamic for regional automotive supply chains.

### [Over 1,500 Academics Sign Letter Supporting Global Inequality Panel](https://www.theguardian.com/inequality/2026/sep/18/global-leaders-urged-to-join-initiative-to-fight-extreme-inequality) ⭐️ 2.0/10

More than 1,500 academics and economists from over 100 countries have signed an open letter urging global leaders to join the International Panel on Inequality. The initiative will be highlighted during the upcoming UN General Assembly in New York. The signatories characterize the widening gap between the richest and poorest as a reversible policy choice rather than an unavoidable outcome. This coordinated effort seeks to establish a high-level, interdisciplinary body dedicated to addressing extreme inequality on a global scale.

rss · 观察家报\(The Observer\) · Sep 18, 06:00

**「What the IPI Is and How It Came Together」** The International Panel on Inequality \(IPI\) is a proposed global body modeled on the Intergovernmental Panel on Climate Change, meant to advise nations on closing the wealth gap between the richest and poorest countries. The initiative was advanced through the Barcelona Declaration of April 2026, and its open letter campaign has gathered signatures from prominent economists such as Thomas Piketty, Daron Acemoglu, and Janet Yellen, along with more than 1,500 academics from over 100 countries.

**「Potential Policy Shift」** The letter aims to pressure world leaders to adopt the International Panel on Inequality, potentially influencing the inclusion of targeted global economic equity measures in UN General Assembly discussions.

President Donald Trump announced an immediate ban on news outlets CNN, MS Now, and Politico from accessing the White House. In a post on Truth Social, the president cited what he described as their &\#x27;constant “reporting” FAKE NEWS\!&\#x27; as the reason for the expulsion. The announcement represents the latest in a series of unprecedented restrictions on major news media access overseen during his second term. Trump also indicated that similar bans could extend to other outlets in the future.

rss · 卫报\(The Guardian\) · Sep 18, 23:04

**「Background」** The First Amendment of the US Constitution protects freedom of the press, which traditionally guarantees journalists the right to access government institutions to report on public affairs. Media bans, where a government administration restricts the accreditation or physical access of specific news outlets, directly challenge this constitutional protection by limiting the ability of certain outlets to report independently on presidential activities.

**「Impact」** Journalists employed by CNN, MS Now, and Politico are effectively prohibited from reporting from within the White House, significantly altering the landscape of presidential press coverage. This action sets a precedent for the government&\#x27;s ability to selectively sanction media organizations based on editorial content.

### [Upskirting and Sexual Harassment at Munich&\#x27;s Oktoberfest Teufelsrad](https://www.spiegel.de/netzwelt/upskirting-beim-oktoberfest-warum-das-teufelsrad-fuer-frauen-gefaehrlich-ist-a-bfc18c2a-d7a3-437c-bed4-1c0f286bee84#ref=rss) ⭐️ 0.0/10

A recent research study highlights that many women visiting the Teufelsrad ride at Munich&\#x27;s Oktoberfest experience their traditional Dirndl dresses slipping up, leading to widespread upskirting. Consequently, videos of these unintentional exposures are frequently uploaded to social media platforms. The article characterizes this phenomenon as a form of sexual harassment that transforms a harmless amusement park attraction into a risk for female attendees. This situation underscores the specific vulnerabilities faced by women at large public events where clothing and ride mechanics can lead to non-consensual exposure.

rss · 明镜周刊\(Der Spiegel\) · Sep 18, 15:09

**「Context of Upskirting」** Upskirting refers to the act of photographing or recording underneath a person&\#x27;s clothing, typically around the waist or crotch area, without their consent. It is a form of sexual violence that often occurs in public spaces where large crowds make it difficult to notice or prevent. The Teufelsrad is a rotating ride at the Oktoberfest in Munich where physical movement and traditional attire can occasionally lead to clothing adjustments that expose the wearer.

**「Impact on Public Safety and Culture」** The widespread availability of non-consensual exposure videos creates a persistent digital trail that undermines the sense of physical and digital security for women attending public festivals. This dynamic pressures event organizers to implement better ride designs, clothing safety standards, or crowd control measures to mitigate the risk of sexual harassment at large-scale cultural events.

**Tags**: `#off-topic`, `#social-issues`, `#public-event`

A column in the Frankfurter Allgemeine Zeitung, titled &quot;Uni Live,&quot; highlights a growing trend where university students feel pressured to prioritize professional experience over their studies. The text notes that merely studying is no longer considered sufficient, as students are expected to gather work experience, complete internships, experiment in the field, and network. The article raises the concern that this dual focus can cause the academic component of the degree to suffer significantly. This perspective is presented by author Lina von Coburg as a critical observation of current career expectations in higher education.

rss · 法兰克福汇报\(Frankfurter Allgemeine Zeitung \(FAZ\)\) · Sep 18, 05:14

**「Background」** Historically, university education was often seen as a standalone path to career readiness, but modern labor markets increasingly demand that graduates demonstrate practical experience alongside their degrees. This shift has led many students to secure internships and part-time jobs concurrently with their coursework to remain competitive, effectively transforming the traditional academic timeline into a multi-tasking arrangement.

Rising temperatures are increasing the demand for cold storage facilities located near urban centers. These facilities are essential for maintaining the required temperature-controlled supply chains for food and pharmaceutical products. The article notes that proximity to cities is making these properties increasingly sought after. A key challenge highlighted is that the significant energy consumption required to maintain these cold environments remains a delicate issue.

**「Supply Chain Context」** Many types of food and medication are temperature-sensitive, meaning they must be kept within specific cold ranges throughout their entire distribution process. This requirement is known as a temperature-controlled or &quot;cold&quot; supply chain, which necessitates specialized real estate such as refrigerated warehouses or cold storage facilities.

**「Property Market Shift」** Real estate investors and logistics operators near cities are facing rising demand and potentially increased property values for cold storage infrastructure, though the high energy costs required to operate these facilities remain a critical financial and environmental constraint.

### [Ahr Valley wine and tourism reinvented five years after the flood](https://www.faz.net/aktuell/reise/fuer-weinbau-und-tourismus-war-die-katastrophe-auch-ein-katalysator-das-ahrtal-fuenf-jahre-nach-der-flut-accg-201210124.html) ⭐️ ?/10

Five years after the catastrophic 2021 Ahr River flood, many impacts in the valley have been addressed, but the disaster served as a catalyst for long-term structural changes in the region. The wine and tourism industries have used this period to rethink their operations and prepare for future challenges, moving away from previous practices that no longer fit modern expectations. This shift represents a decisive change in how the valley approaches its economic pillars.

rss · 法兰克福汇报\(Frankfurter Allgemeine Zeitung \(FAZ\)\) · Sep 18, 11:40

**「Background」** In July 2021, the Ahr region in the Rhineland-Palatinate was struck by a severe flood, one of the most devastating natural disasters in Germany in recent decades. The region is famous for its steep, terraced wine vineyards and a long-standing tourism tradition, both of which were significantly damaged by the flood.

**「Impact」** Winemakers and tourism operators in the Ahr Valley have restructured their business models and infrastructure to improve resilience and competitiveness in the post-flood landscape.

<a id="item-tech-news-346"></a>
### [Global groundwater reserves face critical decline and unpredictability](https://www.faz.net/aktuell/wirtschaft/klima-nachhaltigkeit/kampf-um-das-grundwasser-die-weltweiten-wasserreserven-schrumpfen-accg-201236126.html) ⭐️ ?/10

Global groundwater reserves are experiencing a significant and accelerating shrinkage that threatens long-term water security. Simultaneously, the global water cycle is becoming increasingly unpredictable, making traditional planning and management strategies less effective. The current crisis highlights the urgent need for the development of new solutions and technologies to address this dual challenge. These emerging solutions must account for both the depletion of existing reserves and the volatility of natural hydrological processes to ensure sustainable water management for the future.

rss · 法兰克福汇报\(Frankfurter Allgemeine Zeitung \(FAZ\)\) · Sep 18, 07:01

**「Context」** Groundwater is a critical component of the global water cycle, stored naturally in aquifers beneath the Earth&\#x27;s surface. It serves as a vital freshwater source for drinking water, agriculture, and industrial processes worldwide. A declining global water cycle refers to the increasing variability and unpredictability of natural water distribution and replenishment processes, often exacerbated by climate change and over-extraction.

<a id="item-tech-blog-3"></a>
### [Elon Musk&\#x27;s Behavior Explained Through Political Tribalism and Speculative Diagnosis](https://www.lesswrong.com/posts/oRGEtjivFffdhDHWq/my-current-model-of-what-happened-to-elon-musk) ⭐️ 4.0/10

rss · LessWrong AI风险社区 · Sep 18, 23:15

**「Background」** KatSpartz addresses public speculation about the cause of Elon Musk&\#x27;s erratic online conduct, noting that earlier explanations like sleep deprivation and subclinical bipolar disorder were insufficient to account for the specific content of his recent conspiracy-laden rhetoric.

**「Solution」** The author proposes that political tribalism is the most plausible explanation, arguing that Musk&\#x27;s &\#x27;crazy&\#x27; opinions map directly onto standard MAGA beliefs that became widespread after his 2020 political awakening. She dismisses the bipolar hypothesis as unsupported and the sleep deprivation theory as chronic rather than causal, while acknowledging that chronic ketamine use could contribute to delusions but lacks definitive evidence. She uses data showing a 2025 peak in hostile and conspiratorial posting to support the timeline of political engagement over health issues.

**「Takeaway」** Musk&\#x27;s shift in public persona is best explained by standard political tribalism rather than medical pathology, illustrating that humans routinely adopt &\#x27;crazy&\#x27; beliefs within their chosen social groups without requiring a pathological diagnosis.

**Tags**: `#Political Analysis`, `#Behavioral Psychology`, `#Public Commentary`, `#Speculative Reasoning`

<a id="item-tech-blog-7"></a>
### [Jv Thunder&\#x27;s Reflections on Defining Greatness and Career Focus](https://www.lesswrong.com/posts/dzFuujaW6MLoyTLsc/my-reflections-towards-the-path-to-greatness) ⭐️ ?/10

rss · LessWrong AI风险社区 · Sep 18, 15:46

**「Background」** The author observes a common pressure to accumulate capital early, such as joining quant firms for high salaries, even for interns. He establishes his personal constraints: financial security, a background in winning international olympiads, and a philosophical motivation to prioritize meaningful societal impact over mere wealth accumulation.

**「Solution」** Jv Thunder argues that pursuing deep technical expertise offers greater long-term impact than acquiring capital without specific industry skills. He identifies a personal &quot;ikigai&quot;—an intersection of passion, skill, and societal need—focusing specifically on AI research that makes artificial intelligence safer and more broadly beneficial. He acknowledges that while immediate wealth is tempting, it often leaves a person unskilled for meaningful entrepreneurship; conversely, he commits to a path where deep expertise and discipline, modeled on figures like Hassabis, allow him to build tech companies and nonprofits that serve others.

**「Takeaway」** The core thesis is that true greatness is defined not by external benchmarks like early wealth, but by the dedicated pursuit of deep craft and direct service to humanity. He concludes that in an era of rapid change, avoiding the risk of deep specialization is the greater risk to creating meaningful work.
